# Agents · D · part 29

958 entries, dmf-reviewer to doc-researcher. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **dmf-reviewer**: Read-only Domain Modeling Made Functional reviewer for C# code or a 04-domain-model.md. Finds illegal states that are representable, primitive obsession, missing smart constructors and lifecycle stages, hidden effects, untyped errors, I/O inside domain logic, and leaky or unversioned DTOs, with file:line evidence and C# fixes. Use proactively after domain types or workflows change. ([TutanDev/cc-resources](https://github.com/TutanDev/cc-resources/blob/HEAD/plugins/dmf/agents/dmf-reviewer.md))
- **dmg-cleanup**: Find and fix two code quality issues in damage implementations: 1. **Hardcoded multipliers** — formula multipliers that should use `this.param()` instead of hardcoded numbers 2. **Unnecessary local variables** — `const foo = this.param("X", N)` variables that are only used once or twice and should be inlined ([Anyrainel/GenshinTools](https://github.com/Anyrainel/GenshinTools/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/dmg-cleanup.md) · ★32)
- **dmg-excel**: Cross-validate a character's damage implementation against the Excel community damage calculator (`docs/formulas/原神伤害计算(1).xlsm`). Create tracker items for discrepancies. **Do not modify any implementation code.** ([Anyrainel/GenshinTools](https://github.com/Anyrainel/GenshinTools/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/dmg-excel.md) · ★32)
- **dmg-implement**: Implement `actionable` tracker items for a scope. Write code, run type-check, mark items completed. ([Anyrainel/GenshinTools](https://github.com/Anyrainel/GenshinTools/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/dmg-implement.md) · ★32)
- **dmg-review**: Audit all damage implementations within a scope. Create tracker items for issues found. **This agent does not modify implementation code.** ([Anyrainel/GenshinTools](https://github.com/Anyrainel/GenshinTools/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/dmg-review.md) · ★32)
- **dmg-triage**: Reality-check layer for tracker items created by the review agent. The review agent finds discrepancies between game text and code — this agent decides whether each discrepancy matters. ([Anyrainel/GenshinTools](https://github.com/Anyrainel/GenshinTools/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/dmg-triage.md) · ★32)
- **DMHelper.agent**: Generate and maintain Game Master 5e XML campaign content for Northwatch Wardens: Season One ([jahitchcock/Northwatch-Wardens---HomeBrew-](https://github.com/jahitchcock/Northwatch-Wardens---HomeBrew-/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/DMHelper.agent.md) · ★1)
- **dmidecode-upstream-watcher**: Use this agent on demand to check whether the dmidecode upstreams listed in scripts/upstream_references.json (cloned into the git-ignored c-versions/ by scripts/fetch_upstream_references.py) have changes since their recorded baseline_sha worth porting into libbios, and whether Microsoft's GetSystemFirmwareTable/WMI documentation has changed in a way relevant to win_bios.hpp. Writes a triage report to docs/reviews/. Never edits product code or the manifest. ([yuchdev/SmbiosSdk](https://github.com/yuchdev/SmbiosSdk/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dmidecode-upstream-watcher.md) · ★34)
- **Dmitri**: Dmitri Desgoffe-und-Taxis, antagonist and security specialist. Expert in adversarial testing, breaking assumptions, and finding edge cases. ([asears/grand-ai-hotel](https://github.com/asears/grand-ai-hotel/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/dmitri.agent.md) · ★1)
- **dmitri**: Use this agent when the voices skill needs a backend engineering perspective — API design, data modeling, database schema, performance, failure modes, or system reliability. The person who knows what the runtime does. ([josecriane/nixos-config](https://github.com/josecriane/nixos-config/blob/HEAD/modules/home/develop/claude/agents/dmitry-vyukov.md) · ★1)

- **dmpf-context-author**: Escreve um bounded context completo sobre o kernel DMPF a partir de uma spec de bounded context — esqueleto pelo generator, código dos cinco blocos por agregado, contrato Protobuf e OpenAPI —, roda os gates até passar e para em qualquer gate normativo. - **dn-code-reviewer**: Read-only code reviewer for a diff. Finds correctness bugs, broken contracts, missing tests for changed behavior and risky changes. Used by /dn-review and by the pre-push gate. Ends with a VERDICT line. ([mrtinsjoao/dadosnow-agent-kit](https://github.com/mrtinsjoao/dadosnow-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/kit/.claude/agents/dn-code-reviewer.md))
- **dn-explorer**: Read-only codebase explorer. Use when answering a question means reading more than a handful of files, or when the output would be long and only the conclusion matters. Returns a short report with file paths and line numbers, never file dumps. ([mrtinsjoao/dadosnow-agent-kit](https://github.com/mrtinsjoao/dadosnow-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/kit/.claude/agents/dn-explorer.md))
- **dn-git-ops**: Bounded git operator. Runs one git operation that the caller has fully specified — record exactly the listed files with the given message, push a named branch, or open a pull request. Pushes only when the handoff carries the user's own request. Never decides scope, never merges. ([mrtinsjoao/dadosnow-agent-kit](https://github.com/mrtinsjoao/dadosnow-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/kit/.claude/agents/dn-git-ops.md))
- **dn-monitor**: Read-only project monitor. Reports CI runs, open pull requests with a size and risk triage (via the GitHub CLI) and the recent results of the DadosNow pre-push review gate, as one compact table plus alerts. Never changes anything and never judges the code. ([mrtinsjoao/dadosnow-agent-kit](https://github.com/mrtinsjoao/dadosnow-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/kit/.claude/agents/dn-monitor.md))
- **dn-security-reviewer**: Read-only security reviewer for a diff. Finds committed secrets, injection, broken authorization, unsafe input handling and data exposure. Used by /dn-review and by the pre-push gate. Ends with a VERDICT line. ([mrtinsjoao/dadosnow-agent-kit](https://github.com/mrtinsjoao/dadosnow-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/kit/.claude/agents/dn-security-reviewer.md))

- **dna-construction-logger**: Use PROACTIVELY whenever a specified requirement is about to be, is being, or has just been implemented at a shallower depth than its depth tag requires. Appends a Construction Site entry to 05-CONSTRUCTION-SITES.md capturing the downgrade, affected scenario, and resolution plan. Must be called the moment a simplification is considered — not at code review. ([albertdobmeyer/team-project-scheduler-example](https://github.com/albertdobmeyer/team-project-scheduler-example/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dna-construction-logger.md))
- **dna-cross-checker**: Use PROACTIVELY before a new feature branch is created, and on demand before any PR to main. Parses the "Files this feature will touch" section of every open spec under specs/NNN-*/spec.md and flags overlaps across features. Blocks creation of a branch whose spec claims a file another open branch already claims, unless the shared file is under a configured shared-code path (models/, shared/) in which case it enforces the Article-10 "shared-code PR to main first" rule. ([albertdobmeyer/team-project-scheduler-example](https://github.com/albertdobmeyer/team-project-scheduler-example/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dna-cross-checker.md))

- **dna-spec-auditor**: Use PROACTIVELY at the end of the specification phase, before the first /speckit-specify call on any feature, and on demand before any phase's Experience Audit. Runs the 20+ quality checks from docs/HANDOFF_FORMAT.md against the full 7-doc Blueprint Package (docs/00-CORE-PRINCIPLES.md through docs/05-CONSTRUCTION-SITES.md) plus CONSTITUTION.md. Emits a pass/fail verdict per check with specific file:line references for every failure. Blocks progression if any BLOCKING check fails. ([albertdobmeyer/team-project-scheduler-example](https://github.com/albertdobmeyer/team-project-scheduler-example/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dna-spec-auditor.md))
- **dna-spec-validator**: Use PROACTIVELY after the mechanical dna-spec-validate gate exits 0 (or its findings are accepted), before /dna-test-gate. Detects semantic drift between a per-feature specs/NNN-*/spec.md and the target's 7-doc Blueprint that the script cannot see — negative-assertion violations, non-goal violations, behavioral fidelity to scenario narrative, production-threshold consistency. Runs with FRESH CONTEXT (no carryover from build conversation) per PROJECT_DNA Section 4.3 audit-isolation principle. ([albertdobmeyer/team-project-scheduler-example](https://github.com/albertdobmeyer/team-project-scheduler-example/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dna-spec-validator.md))
- **dna-verifier**: Use PROACTIVELY after /speckit-implement for any feature whose spec contains [D] requirements. Runs the PROJECT_DNA Experience Audit with FRESH context — does not carry over any build-conversation state. Reads spec.md + 01-SYSTEM-INTENT.md scenarios + the implementation from disk, walks through each Experience Fidelity Scenario against the current code, verifies every negative assertion actually holds in the built system, and emits CONGRUENT / DIVERGENT verdict with specific assertion-level failures. ([albertdobmeyer/team-project-scheduler-example](https://github.com/albertdobmeyer/team-project-scheduler-example/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dna-verifier.md))

- **do-not-create-agents-in-install-upgrade-or-background**: `Agent.Create` requires an interactive user session. The platform blocks creation from install codeunits, upgrade codeunits, and background sessions (job queue, scheduled tasks). Packaging an agent in an app does not mean spinning up instances at install. Models still call `Create` from `OnInstallAppPerCompany` to activate the agent.
